<script id="ctl-v22245-theme-json-search-js">(function(){

var memory=null, loadingPromise=null;
function normalize(s){try{return String(s||'').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/đ/g,'d').replace(/[^a-z0-9\s._-]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}catch(e){return String(s||'').toLowerCase().trim();}}
function raw(s){return String(s||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}
function esc(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>'"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;',"'":'&#039;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
function debounce(fn,wait){var t;return function(){var ctx=this,args=arguments;clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(function(){fn.apply(ctx,args);},wait||120);};}
function itemName(it){return it.name||it.title||it.product_name||it.website_product_name||it.post_title||'';}
function itemSku(it){return it.sku||it.product_sku||it._sku||it.model||it.code||'';}
function itemBrand (it){return it.brand||it.brand_name||it.manufacturer||'';}
function itemUrl(it){return it.url||it.product_url||it.permalink||it.link||'#';}
function itemImage(it){return it.image||it.image_url||it.featured_image||it.thumbnail||it.thumb||'';}
function itemPrice(it){return it.price_text||it.price_html||it.display_price||it.sale_price_text||it.regular_text||'';}
function prepare(items){return (items||[]).map(function(it){it=it||{};var kws=Array.isArray(it.keywords)?it.keywords.join(' '):(it.keywords||it.category||it.category_path||'');it._name=normalize(itemName(it));it._sku=normalize(itemSku(it));it._slug=normalize(it.slug||it.post_name||'');it._brand=normalize(itemBrand (it));it._keywords=normalize(kws);it._title=itemName(it);it._sku_raw=itemSku(it);it._brand_raw=itemBrand (it);it._url=itemUrl(it);it._image=itemImage(it);it._price_text=itemPrice(it);return it;});}
function loadIndex(){if(memory){return Promise.resolve(memory);} if(loadingPromise){return loadingPromise;} try{var saved=sessionStorage.getItem('ctl-theme-json-search:'+cfg.indexUrl); if(saved){memory=prepare((JSON.parse(saved).items)||[]); return Promise.resolve(memory);}}catch(e){} loadingPromise=fetch(cfg.indexUrl,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'force-cache'}).then(function(r){if(!r.ok){throw new Error('json_not_found');}return r.json();}).then(function(data){var items=prepare((data&&data.items)?data.items:(data&&data.products?data.products:(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]))); memory=items; try{sessionStorage.setItem('ctl-theme-json-search:'+cfg.indexUrl,JSON.stringify({items:items.slice(0,9000)}));}catch(e){} return items;}).finally(function(){loadingPromise=null;});return loadingPromise;}
function restSearch(q){if(!cfg.restUrl||!window.fetch){return Promise.resolve([]);}var url=cfg.restUrl+'?q='+encodeURIComponent(q)+'&limit='+encodeURIComponent(Math.max(cfg.limit||12,50));return fetch(url,{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store'}).then(function(r){if(!r.ok){throw new Error('rest_failed');}return r.json();}).then(function(data){return prepare((data&&data.items)?data.items:[]);}).catch(function(){return [];});}
function score(it,q,terms){var name=it._name||'',sku=it._sku||'',slug=it._slug||'',brand=it._brand||'',keywords=it._keywords||'',hay=sku+' '+name+' '+slug+' '+brand+' '+keywords;if(!q){return 0;}var s=0;if(sku===q){s+=2000;}else if(sku.indexOf(q)===0){s+=1300;}else if(sku.indexOf(q)>-1){s+=850;}if(name===q){s+=1200;}else if(name.indexOf(q)===0){s+=780;}else if(name.indexOf(q)>-1){s+=540;}if(slug&&slug.indexOf(q)>-1){s+=280;}if(brand&&brand.indexOf(q)>-1){s+=220;}if(keywords&&keywords.indexOf(q)>-1){s+=180;}for(var i=0;i<terms.length;i++){if(!terms[i]){continue;}if(hay.indexOf(terms[i])>-1){s+=70;}else{return 0;}}return s;}
function localSearch(q){var nq=normalize(q),terms=nq.split(' ').filter(Boolean),max=Math.max(cfg.limit||12,50);return loadIndex().then(function(items){var found=[];for(var i=0;i<items.length;i++){var sc=score(items[i],nq,terms);if(sc>0){found.push([sc,items[i]]);}}found.sort(function(a,b){return b[0]-a[0];});return found.slice(0,max).map(function(x){return x[1];});});}
function find(q){return localSearch(q).then(function(items){return items&&items.length?items:restSearch(q);}).catch(function(){return restSearch(q);});}
function searchUrl(q){try{var base=(document.querySelector('link[rel="home"]')||{}).href||window.location.origin+'/';var u=new URL(base,window.location.href);u.searchParams.set('s',q);u.searchParams.set('post_type','product');u.searchParams.set('wp_sync_json_search','1');return u.toString();}catch(e){return '/?s='+encodeURIComponent(q)+'&post_type=product&wp_sync_json_search=1';}}
function render(panel,items,q){var limit=cfg.limit||12;if(!items.length){panel.innerHTML='<div class="wps-theme-json-search-message">'+esc(cfg.emptyText||'Không tìm thấy sản phẩm phù hợp.')+'</div>';panel.classList.add('is-active');return;}var html='<div class="wps-theme-json-search-list">';items.slice(0,limit).forEach(function(it){var name=it._title||it._sku_raw||'Sản phẩm';var img=it._image?'<img class="wps-theme-json-search-thumb" src="'+esc(it._image)+'" alt="'+esc(name)+'" loading="lazy" decoding="async">':'<span class="wps-theme-json-search-thumb" aria-hidden="true"></span>';var meta=[];if(cfg.showSku&&it._sku_raw){meta.push('<span>SKU: <b>'+esc(it._sku_raw)+'</b></span>');}if(cfg.showPrice&&it._price_text){meta.push('<span class="wps-theme-json-search-price">'+esc(it._price_text)+'</span>');}html+='<a class="wps-theme-json-search-item" href="'+esc(it._url)+'">'+img+'<span><span class="wps-theme-json-search-name">'+esc(name)+'</span><span class="wps-theme-json-search-meta">'+meta.join('')+'</span></span><span class="wps-theme-json-search-view">'+esc(cfg.viewText||'Xem sản phẩm')+'</span></a>';});html+='</div>';if(items.length>limit){html+='<div class="wps-theme-json-search-foot"><a href="'+esc(searchUrl(q))+'">'+esc(cfg.allText||'Xem thêm kết quả trong cửa hàng')+'</a></div>';}panel.innerHTML=html;panel.classList.add('is-active');}
function findInput(form){if(!form){return null;}return form.querySelector('input[type="search"],input.search-field,input[name="s"],input[name="q"],input[name="keyword"],input[name="search"],input[name="product_search"],input[name="wps_shop_s"],input[name="ttq"]');}
function badForm(form){try{return !!form.closest('.woocommerce-cart,.woocommerce-checkout,.checkout,.cart,.variations_form,form.cart,.comment-form,#commentform,.wpcf7-form,.mc4wp-form,.login,.register,#adminbarsearch');}catch(e){return false;}}
function matchForm(form){if(!form||form.tagName!=='FORM'||badForm(form)){return false;}var selectors=cfg.selectors||'form.searchform,form.search-form,form[role="search"],form.woocommerce-product-search';try{if(form.matches(selectors)){return true;}}catch(e){try{if(form.matches('form.searchform,form.search-form,form[role="search"],form.woocommerce-product-search')){return true;}}catch(e2){}}
var input=findInput(form);if(!input){return false;}var n=String(input.getAttribute('name')||'').toLowerCase();var t=String(input.getAttribute('type')||'').toLowerCase();var ph=normalize(input.getAttribute('placeholder')||'');return t==='search'||['s','q','keyword','search','product_search','wps_shop_s','ttq'].indexOf(n)>-1||ph.indexOf('tim')>-1||ph.indexOf('search')>-1||ph.indexOf('san pham')>-1;}
function attach(form){if(!matchForm(form)||form.closest('.wp-sync-json-product-search')){return null;}if(form.__wpsJsonSearchApi){return form.__wpsJsonSearchApi;}var input=findInput(form);if(!input||input.type==='hidden'||input.type==='password'){return panel=form.querySelector('.wps-theme-json-search-panel');if(!panel){panel=document.createElement('div');panel.className='wps-theme-json-search-panel';panel.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');form.appendChild(panel);}function doJsonSearch(force){var q=raw(input.value||'');if(q.length<(cfg.minChars||1)&&!force){panel.classList.remove('is-active');return Promise.resolve([]);}if(q.length<1){panel.classList.remove('is-active');return Promise.resolve([]);}panel.innerHTML='<div class="wps-theme-json-search-message">'+esc(cfg.loadingText||'Đang tìm nhanh...')+'</div>';panel.classList.add('is-active');return find(q).then(function(items){render(panel,items,q);return items;});}var api={submit:function(e){var q=raw(input.value||'');if(q.length<1){return false;}if(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation){e.stopImmediatePropagation();}}doJsonSearch(true).then(function(items){if(items.length===1&&items[0]._url&&items[0]._url!=='#'){window.location.href=items[0]._url;return;}window.location.href=searchUrl(q);}).catch(function(){window.location.href=searchUrl(q);});return true;},panel:panel,input:input,search:doJsonSearch};form.__wpsJsonSearchApi=api;var run=debounce(function(){doJsonSearch(false);},110);input.addEventListener('input',run);input.addEventListener('focus',function(){if(raw(input.value||'').length>=(cfg.minChars||1)){doJsonSearch(false);}});form.addEventListener('submit',api.submit,true);return api;}
function init(){var selectors=cfg.selectors||'form.searchform,form[role="search"],form.woocommerce-product-search';var forms=[];try{forms=Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(selectors));}catch(e){forms=[];}forms.forEach(attach);document.addEventListener('focusin',function(e){var f=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('form'):null;if(f){attach(f);}},true);document.addEventListener('submit',function(e){var f=e.target;if(!f||f.tagName!=='FORM'){return;}var api=attach(f);if(api){api.submit(e);}},true);document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key!=='Enter'){return;}var input=e.target;if(!input||input.tagName!=='INPUT'){return;}var f=input.closest?input.closest('form'):null;var api=f?attach(f):null;if(api&&api.input===input){api.submit(e);}},true);document.addEventListener('click',function(e){var f=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('form'):null;if(f){attach(f);}document.querySelectorAll('.wps-theme-json-search-panel.is-active').forEach(function(panel){if(!panel.parentNode||!panel.parentNode.contains(e.target)){panel.classList.remove('is-active');}});},true);}

<script id="wp-sync-theme-font-detector-v22243">(function(){
function cleanFont(v){
v=(v||'').trim();
if(!v||v==='inherit'||v==='initial'||v==='unset'){return '';}
if(/(dashicons|fontawesome|font awesome|fl-icons|icomoon|woocommerce|star|emoji|monospace)/i.test(v)){return '';}
return v;
}
function pickThemeFont(){
var selectors=['body','.site','#page','#wrapper','.page-wrapper','.site-wrapper','.site-content','#content','main','.container'];
for(var i=0;i<selectors.length;i++){
var el=document.querySelector(selectors[i]);
if(!el){continue;}
var font=cleanFont(window.getComputedStyle(el).fontFamily);
if(font){return font;}}
return '';
}
function applyThemeFont(){
document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--wp-sync-theme-font-family',"'Be Vietnam Pro','Be Vietnam',Arial,sans-serif");
}
if(document.readyState==='loading'){
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',applyThemeFont,{once:true});
}else{
applyThemeFont();
}
window.addEventListener('load',applyThemeFont,{once:true});
if(document.fonts&&document.fonts.ready){document.fonts.ready.then(applyThemeFont).catch(function(){});}})();</script>

if(el.classList.contains("woocommerce-error")) return "error";

function extractNotice(el){

function updateCartCount(n){

function addToCart($btn){
var productId=$btn.data('product-id')||$btn.data('product_id')||$btn.attr('data-product_id')||$btn.attr('data-product-id')||0;
var qty=parseInt($btn.data('quantity')||$btn.attr('data-quantity')||1,10)||1;
if(!productId){return false;}
var oldHtml=$btn.html();
$('.wp-sync-cart-inline-notice-notice').hide().text('');
$btn.addClass('is-loading loading').prop('disabled',true).attr('aria-busy','true');
$.ajax({url:cfg.ajaxUrl,type:'POST',dataType:'json',data:{action:'ctl_v1995_add_to_cart',nonce:$btn.data('nonce')||cfg.nonce,product_id:productId,quantity:qty}})
.done(function(res){

$btn.addClass('added').removeClass('loading');
updateCartCount(res.data&&res.data.cart_count);
showToast('success','Đã thêm vào giỏ hàng',(res.data&&res.data.message)?res.data.message:'Sản phẩm đã được thêm vào giỏ hàng.');

}else{

}}).fail(function(xhr){

}).always(function(){
$btn.removeClass('is-loading loading').prop('disabled',false).removeAttr('aria-busy');
if(!$btn.hasClass('added')){$btn.html(oldHtml);}});
return true;
}
